Made by tools/pack_asset_report.py from the pack itself. Names, sizes and counts only.

SCOPE AND LIMITS. This report is an inventory of the payload objects in the pack (meshes, textures, sprites, materials, shaders,
script names, text assets). "ours" is decided by the folder the object is stored under; the optional game check compares names and
vertex counts. It does NOT look inside components. The pack contains COMPONENT SETTINGS COPIED FROM THE STOCK PREFABS OF THE GAME at
build time (EditorUtility.CopySerialized in unity/Editor/*Build.cs, from the Blueprinter placeholder prefabs): particle systems
(muzzle flash, muzzle smoke, contact sparks, wheel dust, sabots, ejection smoke, laser muzzle), audio-source settings, material
parameter blocks (glass, propeller disc), and starting values of other game components. These hold no mesh, texture, audio or code;
they remain the property of Shockfront (CREDITS.md, "The game"). The renderer and material references of those components point out of the pack.
